### [1] [Deepfakes and Synthetic Media: Generation, Detection, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25017)
*Alexandros Gazis,Efstathios Karypidis,Kleanthi Santamouri,Theodoros Vavouras,Nikos E. Mastorakis,Stylianos Pappas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了深度伪造的生成架构、检测方法及治理框架，指出跨生成器泛化是核心挑战，呼吁构建融合取证检测、可验证溯源与制度问责的纵深防御体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造已从技术演示演变为民用与军事领域的关键威胁，可引发身份欺诈、虚假信息传播、证据伪造等严重后果。在新闻、金融、医疗、法律等高风险场景中，其危害延伸至市场操纵与机构信任侵蚀，因此亟需系统梳理检测技术与治理路径。

Method: 论文采用综述方式，梳理生成架构（GAN、自编码器、神经渲染、扩散模型、视频合成），分析其产生的空间、时序、频域与生理伪影，并归纳利用这些伪影的检测器家族（CNN、Transformer、频域方法）。同时考察评估基准与协议，并扩展讨论加密溯源标准、水印与监管框架。

Result: 综述系统梳理了深度伪造生成与检测的技术图景，明确了现有检测方法在跨生成器泛化方面普遍表现不足，并比较了各类评估基准与协议的有效性与局限；同时指出对抗方法不断提升伪造真实性，进一步削弱检测器可靠性。

Conclusion: 跨生成器泛化是该领域的核心开放挑战。有效的深度伪造治理需采取纵深防御策略，将取证检测、可验证来源溯源与制度问责机制相结合，并配合监管框架（如EU AI Act、DSA、GDPR）共同应对民用与军事内容验证需求。

Abstract: Deepfakes, synthetic audiovisual content produced by deep generative models, have escalated into a critical threat across civilian and military domains, enabling identity fraud, disinformation campaigns, and evidence fabrication. In high-stakes environments, ranging from journalism and finance to healthcare and legal contexts, the consequences extend to severe misinformation, market manipulation, identity fraud, and the erosion of institutional trust. This entry explores how modern visual intelligence and computer vision techniques are used to detect deepfakes. It outlines key deepfake generation models, such as GANs, autoencoders, neural rendering, and diffusion systems, while also explaining how adversarial methods enhance realism and challenge existing detectors. The overview highlights visual artifacts, digital patterns, and physiological cues commonly leveraged in detection and reviews major CNN, transformer, and frequency-based approaches. It also summarizes evaluation practices and the difficulty of achieving strong generalization. Finally, it identifies emerging directions, including modern intelligence techniques for civilian and military content verification. This survey covers generation architectures (GANs, latent diffusion, neural rendering, video synthesis), the spatial, temporal, frequency-domain, and physiological artifacts they produce, and the detector families that exploit them. We examine evaluation benchmarks and protocols, highlighting cross-generator generalization as the field's central open challenge. Beyond detection, we discuss cryptographic provenance standards, watermarking, and regulatory frameworks (EU AI Act, DSA, GDPR). We conclude that effective deepfake governance requires defense-in-depth integrating forensic detection, verifiable provenance, and institutional accountability.

</details>


### [2] [SPARC: SuperPixel-Aware Region Contrastive Learning for Self-Supervised Dense Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25067)
*David Szczecina,Yuanpei Xiang,Jitao Hu,David Clausi,Yuhao Chen,Jason Deglint,Paul Fieguth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPARC，一种利用超像素进行区域级对比学习的自监督框架，在分割和检测任务上显著超越已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比式自监督学习方法主要集中在图像级或像素级表示学习，区域级表示学习探索不足。

Method: SPARC利用超像素在增强视图间建立显式对应，引入区域对比分支进行基于超像素的特征池化，并将区域级对比目标与全局图像级目标联合优化。

Result: 相同设置下，SPARC持续优于MoCo-v2和DenseCL，语义分割最高提升+9.79 mIoU，目标检测提升+4.88 AP。消融实验表明区域级目标性能最佳。

Conclusion: 区域级对比学习是提升面向密集预测任务的自监督视觉预训练的有效方法。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has become an effective approach for learning visual representations without manual annotations. Among SSL approaches, contrastive learning has been widely used for visual representation learning. However, existing contrastive SSL methods have focused primarily on image-level or pixel-level representation learning, while region-level representation learning remains less explored. We propose SPARC, a region-level contrastive learning framework that leverages superpixels to establish explicit correspondence between augmented image views. SPARC introduces a region contrastive branch that performs superpixel-based feature pooling and optimizes a region-level contrastive objective jointly with a global image-level objective. Under identical settings, SPARC consistently outperforms previous methods such as MoCo-v2 and DenseCL, achieving improvements of up to +9.79 mIoU for semantic segmentation and +4.88 AP for object detection. Ablation studies further demonstrate that region-level objectives produce the strongest performance. Thus, region-level contrastive learning is an effective approach for improving self-supervised visual pretraining for dense prediction tasks. Code repository can be accessed at https://github.com/xRIPEIx/SPARC.

</details>


### [3] [You've Seen Enough: Quality-Constrained Image Coding for Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25108)
*Khoa Pham-Dinh,Sanaz Nami,Hamed Rezazadegan Tavakoli,Moncef Gabbouj,Farhad Pakdaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将联合压缩-分割训练建模为约束优化问题，通过惩罚函数将人眼观察质量限制在目标水平，把剩余码率用于提升机器任务性能，实现显著BD-rate下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉数据越来越多由机器视觉系统而非人类消费。现有ICM编码虽以计算机为主要观察者，但人类仍需检查或验证决策，因此人眼质量不可完全忽略。受"恰可察觉失真"启发，作者希望将人眼质量恰好限制在可接受水平，从而把剩余编码能力用于提升机器任务性能，而非无限制地提升人眼质量。

Method: 将联合压缩-分割训练重新表述为带约束的优化问题：编解码器需满足预设的可接受目标视觉质量，任务项消耗剩余编码容量。为此设计惩罚函数引导质量趋向目标值，提出两种惩罚函数——绝对函数和双线性函数，后者在超过目标视觉质量后施加更陡的斜率以更强约束。该方法无需增加额外复杂度。

Result: 在质量约束下，所提方法相比无约束的联合率-失真-任务优化取得-22.82%的BD-rate，相比简单率-失真基线取得-29.81%的BD-rate，即在相同任务性能下降低码率。同时编解码器以合理误差达到目标视觉质量，且未增加任何复杂度开销。

Conclusion: 通过将人眼质量约束在目标水平并释放剩余码率给机器任务，可在保证可接受视觉质量的前提下显著降低码率并维持任务性能。双线性惩罚函数因超目标后更陡的斜率而更有效，方法在无额外复杂度下同时满足质量约束与任务性能需求。

Abstract: Visual data is increasingly consumed by machine-vision systems rather than by human observers. Image Coding for Machines (ICM) compresses images assuming the main observer is a computer vision application and that the human observer needs to inspect or validate the decisions. Inspired by just-noticeable distortion, which sets the quality to the just-acceptable level for human observers, we aim to cap the human-observed quality at a desired level, with the goal of using the remaining coding capacity to improve the machine performance. We recast joint compression-segmentation training as a constrained optimization problem in which the codec must meet a predefined acceptable target visual quality while a task term consumes the remaining coding capacity. We solve this by designing a penalty function to guide the quality to the desired target. We propose two penalty functions, an absolute function and a bilinear function, the latter applying a steeper slope once the target visual quality is exceeded. Experimental results show that, under the quality constraint, the proposed method achieves a BD-rate of $-22.82\%$ over an unconstrained joint rate--distortion--task optimization and $-29.81\%$ over a simple rate--distortion baseline, showcasing bitrate reduction with the same task performance. This is achieved while the codec also meets the target visual quality with a reasonable error and without adding any complexity overhead.

</details>


### [4] [Geometric and Semantic Coupling for Interaction Understanding in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25247)
*Hanyang Kong,Xingyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Segment-Snap，通过部件与把手的物理关系连接可动部件、运动与操作区域，实现 3D 场景交互理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 场景的交互理解需要同时描述可动部件、其运动方式以及可供操作的区域，但现有方法难以将这些输出有效关联。

Method: 用学习到的预测器识别部件表面与把手；几何解码器利用平面与直立先验约束运动，并依据预测把手位置选择铰链线（不训练运动回归器）；联合部件-把手预测器提供额外把手候选，并用所属部件修正其运动类别；每种信息传递仅执行一次，无迭代反馈。

Result: 在 Articulate3D 验证集上，固定掩码与轴时，把手引导使运动门控 AP 从 13.74% 提升至 40.98%；额外把手候选使把手 AP 从 24.63% 提升至 29.65%，基于部件的类别修正再增 0.98 点，完整上下文达 30.99%。

Conclusion: 重复训练、学习解码器对照与配对可视化验证了结合几何与语义证据在交互理解中的优势与局限。

Abstract: Interaction understanding in 3D scenes requires a joint description of movable parts, their motion, and the regions through which they can be operated. We present Segment-Snap, which connects these outputs through the physical relationship between parts and handles. Learned predictors identify broad part surfaces and small handles. A geometric decoder uses planar and upright priors to constrain motion, then selects hinge lines using predicted handle locations, without training a motion regressor. Conversely, a joint part-and-handle predictor supplies additional handle candidates, whose motion classes are refined using containing parts. Each information transfer is applied once, without iterative feedback. On Articulate3D validation, handle guidance raises motion-gated AP from 13.74% to 40.98% at fixed masks and axes. Additional handle candidates raise handle AP from 24.63% to 29.65%; part-based class correction adds 0.98 points, and full context reaches 30.99%. Repeated training, learned-decoder controls and paired visualizations establish the benefits and limitations of combining geometric and semantic evidence for interaction understanding.

</details>


### [5] [ImIR: Image-Instruction Tuning for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25267)
*Süleyman Aslan,Görkay Aydemir,Mısra Yavuz,Yunus Bilge Kurt,Nasrin Rahimi,Ahmet Rasim Emirdağı,Burak Can Biner,M. Akın Yılmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文用从退化图像自身导出的连续"图像指令"替代文本提示，通过轻量token mapper将退化图像的视觉-语言嵌入推向干净图像嵌入，用单一低秩适配器在三小时内、单GPU上让Qwen-Image-Edit模型适配六种复原任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像退化类型多样，实用的复原系统需用单一模型处理多种退化。近期有效方案是用小型低秩适配器加文本提示，将大型预训练图像编辑模型适配到复原任务，但文本提示依赖人工书写且需要退化标签。

Method: 用退化图像本身导出的"图像指令"替代文本提示。图像经两条路径进入编辑器：结构来自模型VAE，语义指令来自轻量token mapper，该mapper把退化图像的视觉-语言嵌入向干净图像的嵌入方向移动。因指令是连续向量，对其缩放可为低光增强等目标不唯一的任务生成一系列有效复原结果。使用单一适配器训练。

Result: 单一适配器在一张GPU上约三小时训练完成，使Qwen-Image-Edit模型适配六种任务。在匹配对比下，图像指令优于文本条件；且无需退化标签即可支持任务无关复原，而文本变体做不到这一点。

Conclusion: 图像指令是一种优于文本条件的高效通用复原条件方式，支持无退化标签的任务无关复原，并能通过连续向量缩放为不唯一目标任务提供多样化解。

Abstract: Degradations vary widely across images, so a practical restoration system has to handle many degradation types with one model. A recent and effective recipe adapts a large pretrained image-editing model to restoration using a small low-rank adapter with a text prompt. We replace that prompt with an instruction derived from the degraded image itself. The image reaches the editor through two paths: its structure comes from the model's VAE, and its semantic instruction comes from a lightweight token mapper that shifts the degraded image's vision-language embedding toward the embedding a clean image would produce. Because the instruction is a continuous vector, scaling it yields a family of valid restorations for tasks whose target is not unique, such as low-light enhancement. We adapt one Qwen-Image-Edit model to six tasks with a single adapter trained in about three hours on one GPU. The image instruction outperforms text conditioning under a matched comparison, and it supports task agnostic restoration without a degradation label, which the text variant does not.

</details>


### [6] [RULER: Instance-aware Rubric Rewards for SVG Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25270)
*Hangyu Ran,Yuhao Zheng,Yingying Zhang,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Han Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对自然语言生成SVG代码任务缺乏可靠评价信号的问题，提出基于评分量规（rubric）的评估与强化学习方法RULER，通过实例化多轴rubric打分并用于强化学习优化，显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVG生成任务开放性强、无绝对视觉真值，标量指标（CLIP、Aesthetic）在自然图像上校准，难以迁移到风格化矢量内容，用作RL奖励会引发奖励劫持（reward hacking），因此评估与策略优化都缺乏可靠信号。

Method: 先通过实验证明：用多轴rubric提示视觉语言评判模型，其与人类判断的相关性远高于标量指标。基于此提出RULER（Instance-aware Rubric Rewards for Reinforcement Learning）：将每条指令转化为包含语义、视觉、风格三类共六个条目的实例化rubric，由评判VLM对渲染后的rollout逐条打分，加权满意度形成细粒度奖励，采用GRPO优化。该rubric仅由文本派生，无需配对SVG真值或人类偏好标注。

Result: 在MMSVG-Illustration和MMSVG-Icon上，RULER将rubric得分从0.432/0.395提升至0.693/0.683，超越专用SVG模型，并与规模大得多的DeepSeek-V3相当；消融实验表明rubric设计是开放式SVG生成中RL的有效杠杆。

Conclusion: 基于rubric的评分可同时解决SVG生成的评估与奖励优化问题，RULER在无需真值标签的情况下显著提升生成质量，说明rubric设计是开放生成任务中RL的关键因素。

Abstract: Generating Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) code from natural-language instructions is an open-ended task without absolute visual ground truth, leaving both evaluation and policy optimization without a faithful signal. Scalar metrics (CLIP, Aesthetic) calibrated on natural images transfer poorly to stylized vector content, and reusing them as RL rewards triggers reward hacking. We address both limitations with rubric-based scoring. We first establish empirically that prompting a vision-language judge with a multi-axis rubric correlates with human judgments far better than scalar metrics, both across samples and within instructions. Building on this finding, we introduce RULER (Instance-aware Rubric Rewards for Reinforcement Learning), which converts each instruction into an instance-aware rubric of six items spanning semantic, visual, and stylistic axes; a judge VLM scores rendered rollouts item-by-item, and the weighted satisfactions form a fine-grained reward optimized via Group Relative Policy Optimization. Because the rubric is derived from text alone, RULER requires neither paired SVG ground truth nor human preference labels. On MMSVG-Illustration and MMSVG-Icon, RULER lifts the rubric score from 0.432/0.395 to 0.693/0.683, surpassing dedicated SVG specialists and matching the substantially larger DeepSeek-V3, with ablations identifying rubric design as the active lever for RL on open-ended SVG generation. The project page is available at https://hangyuran.github.io/RULER/.

</details>


### [7] [Uncertainty-Aware 3D Residual Wavelet Diffusion for Ultra Low-Field MRI Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25319)
*Rui W. Yeow,Millie Beament,Fred Dick,Raha Razin,Martina Bocchetta,David L. Thomas,Henry F. J. Tregidgo,Daniel C. Alexander,James H. Cole*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种3D残差小波扩散模型，用于超低场MRI全脑超分辨率重建，在保持体积学精度的同时提供逐体素不确定性图和更好的扫描仪泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低场MRI可扩大神经影像的可及性，但信噪比低、对比度差、层厚大。基于回归的超分辨虽能恢复解剖细节，却只给出单一确定性估计，无法指示低场输入解剖信息不确定的区域。生成式扩散模型可采样后验分布来量化这种不确定性，但应用于3D全脑MRI时受限于内存瓶颈、采样慢和扫描仪域偏移。

Method: 提出3D残差小波扩散模型，结合三点：无损小波重参数化将空间网格缩小以在单GPU上容纳全脑；残差 shifting 从低场输入出发加速采样；域随机化在无配对训练数据下提升扫描仪泛化。由于高场参考并非体素对齐的真值，评估下游体积一致性。

Result: 在健康队列（n=19，0.064T与3T成像）上，本方法在体积精度上匹配领先的通用回归方法，同时额外生成逐体素不确定性图以突出不确定区域。在认知障碍参与者试点数据集（n=11）上，疾病相关萎缩得以保留，而非被归一化到健康先验。

Conclusion: 该框架在全脑后验采样与低场超分辨率之间取得平衡，且不牺牲体积精度。

Abstract: Ultra low-field MRI expands global access to neuroimaging but produces scans with low signal-to-noise ratio, reduced contrast, and thick slices. While regression-based super-resolution can recover anatomical detail for segmentation, it returns a single deterministic estimate that gives no indication of regions where the low-field input leaves anatomy underdetermined. Generative diffusion models offer an alternative by sampling the posterior distribution of plausible high-field images, quantifying this anatomical ambiguity. However, applying them to 3D whole-brain MRI is restricted by memory bottlenecks, slow sampling, and scanner domain shifts. We propose a 3D residual wavelet diffusion model that combines three ideas to overcome these hurdles. A lossless wavelet reparameterisation shrinks the spatial grid to fit a whole brain on a single GPU, residual shifting accelerates sampling by starting from the low-field input, and domain randomisation promotes scanner generalisation without paired training data. As the high-field reference is not a voxel-aligned ground truth, we evaluate downstream volumetric agreement. On a healthy cohort (n=19) imaged at 0.064T and 3T, our method matches a leading general-purpose regression approach in volumetric accuracy while additionally generating per-voxel uncertainty maps highlighting underdetermined regions. Furthermore, on a pilot dataset (n=11) of participants with cognitive impairment, disease-relevant atrophy is preserved rather than normalised towards a healthy prior. Our framework brings whole-brain posterior sampling to low-field super-resolution without sacrificing volumetric accuracy.

</details>


### [8] [MirrorDistill: Illumination-Aware Latent Distillation for Efficient Low-Light Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25331)
*Farida Mohsen,Tala Zaim,Nurul Izni Rusli,Ali Al-Zawqari,Ali Safa,Samir Brahim Belhaouari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MirrorDistill，一种光照感知的潜在蒸馏框架，通过特征镜像将低光域与干净域关联，在训练中对学生网络进行逐层多尺度对齐，仅需轻量学生网络推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低光图像增强方法多依赖输出级重建损失，只监督最终恢复图像，导致中间特征恢复过程约束较弱。

Method: 训练时用共享编码器和EMA教师解码器处理干净参考图，生成干净域潜在目标，对低光学生在原始编码器特征和标准化多尺度解码器投影两个层级逐层对齐，并采用光照感知加权方案更侧重欠曝区域。教师与参考分支仅在训练时使用。

Result: 在标准LOL基准上，MirrorDistill在真实拍摄的LOL-v2-Real集上超越SOTA，且计算复杂度（GMACs）最低；在LOL-v1和LOL-v2-Synthetic上保持竞争力。消融实验验证了编码器镜像、解码器镜像和光照感知加权的贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架在不增加推理期教师侧计算成本的前提下提升低光增强性能，并已开源代码以利于后续研究。

Abstract: Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) is an im- portant component of visual sensing systems operating under degraded illumination, including nighttime surveillance, au- tonomous navigation, remote sensing, and inspection in poorly lit industrial environments. Most LLIE methods rely on output- level reconstruction losses that supervise only the final restored image, leaving the intermediate feature recovery process weakly constrained. This paper proposes MirrorDistill, an illumination- aware latent distillation framework that links the low-light and clean domains through feature mirroring. During training, a shared encoder and an exponential-moving-average teacher decoder process the clean reference image to generate clean- domain latent targets. These targets supervise the low-light student at two levels: raw encoder features and standardized multi-scale decoder projections. The alignment is applied layer by layer, while a proposed illumination-aware weighting scheme gives greater emphasis to underexposed regions. The teacher and reference branches are used only during training, so inference requires only the lightweight student encoder-decoder and in- troduces no teacher-side computational cost. Under evaluation on the standard LOL benchmarks, MirrorDistill outperforms the state-of-the-art methods on the real-captured LOL-v2-Real set, while having the lowest compute complexity (GMACs) and while remaining competitive on the LOL-v1 and LOL-v2-Synthetic datasets. Ablation studies further show the contributions of the encoder mirror, decoder mirror, and illumination-aware weighting. Finally, we release our code as open-source for the benefit of future research.

</details>


### [9] [Sex Estimation from Footwear Outsole Impressions Using CNN Transfer Learning and Interpretable Image Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25386)
*Jinyi Niu,Ziyi Song,Weining Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究比较了CNN迁移学习与传统特征方法在鞋底印痕二分类性别估计中的表现，发现微调CNN性能最强，并揭示了学习表征与可测量图像属性之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鞋底印痕是常见的法庭模式证据，但从这些图像中定量估计穿着者属性的方法仍相对不成熟，需要探索更有效的定量手段。

Method: 使用公开鞋底印痕数据集，按鞋级别划分训练与测试集（同一物理鞋的重复扫描归入同侧以减少数据泄漏）；评估方式包括预训练CNN端到端微调、冻结特征提取加SVM分类，以及融合手工、几何与元数据描述符的混合特征方法。

Result: 微调CNN总体预测性能最强，显著优于仅使用人工指定描述符的传统分类器；冻结特征方法提供了计算成本更低的替代方案；对低维CNN表征的探索性分析显示其与频率阈值比、图像对比度及小波摘要存在关联。

Conclusion: CNN迁移学习能够捕捉超出所考虑描述符的判别信息，为基于鞋类的法庭筛查提供了有前景的方法；在投入实际应用前，仍需在独立采集及类案件条件下进一步验证。

Abstract: Footwear outsole impressions are a common form of forensic pattern evidence, yet quantitative methods for estimating wearer attributes from these images remain relatively underdeveloped. We investigate binary sex estimation from footwear outsole impressions by comparing convolutional neural network (CNN) transfer learning with traditional feature-based classification. Using a publicly available outsole-impression dataset, we adopt a shoe-level training and test partition that keeps replicate scans of the same physical shoe together to reduce data leakage. We evaluate pretrained CNNs through end-to-end fine-tuning, frozen feature extraction followed by support vector machine classification, and hybrid feature fusion incorporating handcrafted, geometric, and metadata-derived descriptors. Fine-tuned CNNs achieve the strongest overall predictive performance and substantially outperform traditional classifiers trained on the manually specified descriptors alone, while frozen-feature approaches offer a less computationally demanding alternative. Exploratory analysis of low-dimensional CNN representations reveals associations with frequency threshold ratio, image contrast, and wavelet-based summaries, providing a connection between learned representations and measurable properties of outsole impressions. These findings suggest that CNN transfer learning captures discriminative information beyond the descriptors considered and offers a promising approach to footwear-based forensic screening. Further validation on independently collected and casework-like impressions is needed before operational use.

</details>


### [10] [Directional Total Variation-Regularized Implicit Neural Representations (DTV-INR) for Continuous Super-Resolution in Degraded Imaging Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25429)
*Mahmoud Saeedi Kelishami*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DTV-INR，将SIREN隐式神经网络与各向异性结构张量引导的方向全变分正则结合，用于分辨率无关图像超分，理论证明适定性并在MRI与电镜数据上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续到离散的采集过程本质是不适定逆问题，传统隐式神经表示（INR）缺乏有效正则约束，而标量全变分（TV）方案易产生阶梯伪影。因此需要一个能自适应图像结构方向、且分辨率无关的正则化范式。

Method: 构建DTV-INR变分框架：以SIREN架构坐标网络为基础，引入动态黎曼度量张量场D(x)（源自结构张量），通过其谱分解使扩散优先沿主结构轮廓方向，惩罚跨边缘耗散。在H^1(Omega)空间证明极小元的存在性、唯一性与度量稳定性，并用交替投影优化算法解耦网络参数更新与自适应张量场更新。

Result: 在临床脑部MRI与生物医学透射电镜数据上，连续（非整数）上采样因子下PSNR较无正则INR基线提升最多+5.05 dB，较各向同性TV-INR提升+1.71–2.85 dB；噪声鲁棒性达sigma_eta=0.10，且呈现单调预条件收敛行为。

Conclusion: DTV-INR通过结构张量引导的各向异性TV正则有效规避阶梯伪影，兼具理论适定性保证与优异的定量、定性超分性能及噪声鲁棒性，适用于分辨率无关的医学与生物成像超分任务。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the Directional Total Variation-Regularized Implicit Neural Representation (DTV-INR), an advanced variational paradigm that synergistically integrates coordinate-driven implicit neural networks with an anisotropic, structure-tensor-informed total variation regularizer tailored for resolution-agnostic image super-resolution. Casting the continuous-to-discrete acquisition process into an ill-posed inverse problem framework, our formulation equips a SIREN-architected coordinate network with a dynamic Riemannian metric tensor field D(x). By leveraging its spectral decomposition, the proposed regularizer preferentially directs diffusion parallel to dominant structural contours while penalizing cross-edge dissipation, successfully circumventing the classical staircasing artifacts inherent to scalar total variation schemes. We rigorously prove the well-posedness of this formulation in H^1(Omega) by establishing the existence, uniqueness, and metric stability of the variational minimizer, and realize this via an alternating projected optimization algorithm that decouples network parameter tuning from adaptive tensor field updates. Comprehensive experiments conducted on clinical brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) and biomedical transmission electron microscopy confirm substantial quantitative and qualitative improvements, yielding PSNR enhancements reaching +5.05 dB over baseline unregularized INRs and +1.71-2.85 dB over isotropic TV-INR across continuous (non-integer) upsampling factors, alongside remarkable noise robustness up to sigma_eta = 0.10 and monotonic preconditioned convergence behavior.

</details>


### [11] [Combinatorial Network-Based Manifold Topological Deep Learning for Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25453)
*Alice Wachira,Xiang Liu,Zhe Su,Yiying Tong,Ge Wang,Guo-Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CNMTDL框架，将Hodge分解与组合注意力机制结合用于医学图像分析，并在MedMNIST v2的六个数据集上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像具有复杂的几何与拓扑结构，而传统卷积神经网络将图像视为规则欧氏网格，难以保留几何关系和高阶结构信息。流形拓扑深度学习（MTDL）虽具潜力，但现有方法尚未充分利用组合复形神经网络中的离散流形结构。

Method: 将医学图像表示为离散流形并分解为三个Hodge分量；从这些分量中提取的特征被拼接并嵌入组合复形架构，通过基于注意力的模块增强0-cell与2-cell之间的高阶消息传递。

Result: 在MedMNIST v2基准的六个二维和三维数据集上进行评估，证明了该方法在医学图像分析中的有效性。

Conclusion: CNMTDL通过融合Hodge分解与组合注意力机制，有效利用离散流形结构，为医学图像分析提供了一种有前景的流形拓扑深度学习方案。

Abstract: Medical image analysis remains fundamentally challenging because of the intricate geometric and topological structures present in medical data. Conventional convolutional neural networks model images as regular Euclidean grids, limiting their ability to preserve geometric relationships and higher-order structural information. Recently, manifold topological deep learning (MTDL) has emerged as a promising paradigm that integrates deep learning with geometric and topological representations. Nevertheless, existing methods have not yet fully exploited discrete manifold structures within combinatorial complex neural networks. To bridge this gap, we introduce CNMTDL, a MTDL framework that integrates Hodge decomposition with a combinatorial attention mechanism. In our approach, medical images are represented as discrete manifolds and decomposed into three Hodge components. Features extracted from these components are concatenated and embedded into a combinatorial complex architecture, enabling enhanced higher-order message passing between $0$-cells and $2$-cells through attention-based blocks. We evaluate CNMTDL on six two-dimensional and three-dimensional datasets from the MedMNIST v2 benchmark, demonstrating its effectiveness for medical image analysis.

</details>


### [12] [MIND the Gap: A Geographic Implicit Neural Representation with Adjustable Spatial Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25454)
*Isaac Corley,Arjun Rao,Esther Rolf,Konstantin Klemmer,Evan Shelhamer,Nils Lehmann,Marc Rußwurm,Gengchen Mai,Nathan Jacobs,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MIND（Matryoshka隐式神经蒸馏），将多个专家地理空间模型的嵌入蒸馏为单一通用坐标嵌入，并支持可调空间粒度；同时构建CoordBench大规模评测套件，在区域外推等任务上取得地理隐式神经表示的新最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理测量数据稀疏，大量区域缺乏标签。地理隐式神经表示(INRs)可学习平滑通用的嵌入，在任意坐标查询，使下游模型在推理时无需卫星影像即可预测未采样位置的目标值。然而，现有方法对遥远区域的泛化能力仍缺乏探索，而这在遥感应用中至关重要。

Method: 提出Matryoshka隐式神经蒸馏(MIND)：将专家预训练地理空间模型的嵌入蒸馏到单一通用坐标嵌入中，具备可调空间粒度。采用多嵌入维度的嵌套监督，形成一系列连续块(chunks)。下游预测器可仅保留前导块，或使用Chunked Penalty在保留完整嵌入的同时降低后续块权重，且无需重新训练INR。并构建CoordBench评测套件，涵盖52个数据集、78个目标，测试不同空间尺度下的局部插值与留出区域预测。

Result: 实验显示早期块捕获较粗粒度地理变化，后续块补充更细粒度细节。MIND及Chunked Penalty变体在测试的INR中取得最高聚合回归与分类分数，并在区域留出(regional holdout)设定下取得整体最高分。

Conclusion: MIND为地理隐式神经表示树立了新的最优水平，验证了多专家蒸馏与可调粒度嵌入在跨区域泛化上的有效性，CoordBench也为大规模INR评测提供了标准基准。

Abstract: Geographic measurements are often sparse, leaving large areas without labels for the quantities we want to map. Geographic implicit neural representations (INRs) address this by learning smooth, general-purpose embeddings that can be queried at any coordinate. Downstream models combine these embeddings with sparse labels to predict target values at unsampled locations without satellite imagery at inference. However, generalization to distant regions remains largely unexplored, despite its importance for remote sensing applications. We introduce Matryoshka Implicit Neural Distillation (MIND), which distills embeddings from specialist pretrained geospatial models into a single generalist coordinate embedding with adjustable spatial granularity. MIND uses nested supervision at several embedding dimensions, which define a series of contiguous chunks. In our experiments, early chunks capture coarser geographic variation, while later chunks add more fine-grained details. A downstream predictor can retain only leading chunks or be fitted with our Chunked Penalty to downweight later chunks while keeping the full embedding, without retraining the INR. To measure MIND and compare to existing approaches around the world, we introduce CoordBench, a large-scale INR evaluation suite of $52$ datasets and $78$ targets that aims to test both local interpolation and prediction in held-out regions at various spatial scales. MIND and its Chunked Penalty variant achieve the highest aggregate regression and classification scores among tested INRs, and the highest scores overall under regional holdout, setting a new state-of-the-art for geographic INRs.

</details>


### [13] [SAM-V: Geometry-Aware Segment Anything for Multi-View Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25490)
*Jiangshan Gong,Yuqun Wu,Qiqian Fu,Yao Xiao,Chuhang Zou,Shenlong Wang,Derek Hoiem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAM-V，将几何模型VGGT特征直接集成进SAM分割模型，端到端训练实现无需离线匹配的多视角一致实例分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角物体分割在显著视角和遮挡变化下仍具挑战。现有方法要么在点云上做3D实例分割（受限于稀缺的3D标注），要么依赖离线2D掩码匹配流程（存在跨帧物体身份歧义）。

Method: SAM-V将前馈几何模型（VGGT）的特征直接整合进2D分割基础模型（SAM），端到端训练进行跨视角实例预测。引入提示融合机制，用视角特定的相机token与局部VGGT特征增强稀疏的SAM提示token，使提示表示既具视角感知又具空间接地；同时设计掩码解码器关注密集的2D与3D特征。

Result: 在IGGT 3D跟踪基准上，SAM-V在ScanNet++划分上相比最先进多视角实例分割基线，整体IoU提升5个点、帧级召回率提升12个点；在零样本ScanNet划分上所有指标均领先。

Conclusion: 通过将掩码解码直接以多视角几何为条件，SAM-V在单次前向传播中即可产出被提示物体的多视角一致分割，无需离线掩码匹配或显式3D重建。代码与预训练模型已开源。

Abstract: Consistent multi-view object segmentation is critical for 3D perception and robotics, yet remains challenging under severe viewpoint and occlusion changes. Existing methods typically perform 3D instance segmentation on point clouds or rely on offline 2D mask-matching pipelines. However, 3D instance segmentation is limited by scarce 3D annotations, while offline 2D matching suffers from object identity ambiguity across frames. To leverage strong 2D and 3D priors jointly, we propose SAM-V (Geometry-Aware Segment Anything for Multi-View Instance Segmentation). Instead of combining the two priors through post-hoc matching, SAM-V directly integrates features from a feed-forward geometry model (VGGT) into a 2D segmentation foundation model (SAM), trained end-to-end for cross-view instance prediction. SAM-V introduces a prompt-fusion mechanism that enriches sparse SAM prompt tokens with view-specific camera tokens and local VGGT features, making the prompt representation both view-aware and spatially grounded, together with a mask decoder that attends to dense 2D and 3D features. By conditioning the mask decoding directly on multi-view geometry, SAM-V produces consistent multi-view segmentation of a prompted object in a single forward pass without offline mask matching or explicit 3D reconstruction. On the IGGT 3D tracking benchmark, where consistent instance identity across frames directly determines performance, SAM-V improves overall IoU by 5 points and frame-level recall by 12 points on the ScanNet++ split over the state-of-the-art multi-view instance segmentation baseline and leads on all metrics in the zero-shot ScanNet split. Our code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/gong208/SAM-V.git.

</details>


### [14] [RGSQ: Riemannian Geometry-Sensitive Quantization for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25492)
*Zhiping Wu,Dongdong Ren,Yangchengyu Zhou,Zhengjie Zhang,Wenbin Li,Hongbing Pan,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RGSQ，用统一Fisher-Riemannian度量将VLM量化建模为重构问题，在极低位（W2A8/W3A8）下取得最优精度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法多面向单模态LLM，将量化误差视为欧氏假设下的各向同性扰动，无法有效指示VLM中各模态的敏感方向；直接迁移或仅做模态特定缩放会导致比特分配不均和低位下性能不一致。

Method: RGSQ将量化形式化为统一Fisher-Riemannian度量下的重构问题：通过模态分区的经验Fisher因子构建Riemannian流形映射，识别模态特定敏感方向，并融合为模态感知的Kronecker结构度量；再施加几何对齐旋转重定向局部切空间框架，使低位扰动转向损失不敏感轴；最后用白化变换将Riemannian目标映射为等价欧氏形式，使标准单模态PTQ方法可在原假设下评估多模态量化误差。

Result: 在多种主流VLM基准上，RGSQ在极低位（W2A8、W3A8）取得最高精度和稳定性，较MBQ、MQuant等VLM感知基线最高提升5.9%，较单模态改进最高提升8.6%。

Conclusion: 引入Riemannian几何敏感度可有效统一多模态量化误差度量，兼容现有单模态PTQ流程，并在极低位VLM部署中实现领先精度与稳定性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (VLMs) can be efficiently deployed under stringent memory and latency constraints through post training quantization (PTQ). However, most PTQ methods are designed for unimodal large language models (LLMs). These methods treat quantization errors as isotropic perturbations under the Euclidean assumption, which provides weak guidance on directions most sensitive to quantization in VLMs. Consequently, directly adapting unimodal PTQ approaches or solely employing modality-specific scaling often leads to uneven bit-width distribution and inconsistent performance in low-bit settings. To address these challenges, we propose Riemannian Geometry-Sensitive Quantization (RGSQ), which formulates quantization as a reconstruction problem under a unified Fisher-Riemannian metric. RGSQ identifies modality-specific sensitive directions via Riemannian manifold mappings built from modality-partitioned empirical Fisher factors and fused into a modality-aware Kronecker-structured metric. We then apply geometry-aligned rotations to reorient the local tangent frame, steering low-bit perturbations toward loss-insensitive axes. Finally, we apply a whitening transformation that maps the Riemannian objective to an equivalent Euclidean form, enabling standard unimodal PTQ methods to evaluate multimodal quantization error under their original assumptions. Across an extensive and diverse set of mainstream VLM benchmarks, RGSQ achieves the highest accuracy and stability under extremely low-bit settings (W2A8 and W3A8). It outperforms VLM-aware baselines, such as MBQ and MQuant, by up to 5.9% and surpasses single-modality improvements by up to 8.6%.

</details>


### [15] [mbariml: a curation pipeline for turning deep-sea imagery and video into object-detection training data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25500)
*Lonny Lundsten,Kevin Barnard,Dave Caress*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了名为mbariml的Python视频与图像分析流程，围绕数据标注管理构建，将YOLO检测结果转化为可批量人工审核的ROI，并输出为训练数据与元数据，以实现深海目标检测性能的迭代提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深海视频与图像中的目标（主要为生物）稀疏、模糊且难以识别，训练数据的数量与质量直接影响目标检测性能；因此需要一种迭代式的数据标注与管理方法来逐步改善检测器性能。

Method: mbariml使用Ultralytics YOLO检测模型对静态图像或视频运行检测，将每个检测结果存储为可审核的感兴趣区域（ROI），按视觉相似性对ROI分组以便人工批量接受或拒绝，并导出为训练数据、统计信息、图像旁车文件及额外元数据。人工审核为核心环节，标注者可验证、重标注、调整尺寸、删除或新增定位框，所有编辑均写回检测器所用的同一数据库。视频方面，将每个跟踪器生成的轨迹视为临时观测，仅选取一个代表帧而非保留轨迹中的全部检测，并采用"中间三分之一"启发式进行轨迹观测选择。

Result: 论文以逐阶段方式描述了该流程，包括用于轨迹观测选择的操作性"中间三分之一"启发式方法；系统能够支持检测结果的分组批量审核、人工编辑回写数据库，以及训练数据与元数据的导出。

Conclusion: mbariml以人工审核为中心，构建了面向深海图像与视频的迭代式标注与管理流程，通过视觉相似性分组批量审核和轨迹代表帧选择降低标注成本，并将人工编辑与检测结果统一存于同一数据库，为持续改进目标检测器性能提供数据基础。

Abstract: Training data quantity and quality greatly affect object detection model performance, regardless of model architecture. When using object detection models on video and images from the deep sea, in which the objects of interest, primarily organisms, are sparse, faint, and hard to identify, incremental improvements to object detector performance may require an iterative approach to data labeling and management. This paper presents mbariml, a python-based video and image analysis pipeline built around the data labeling management process. mbariml uses an Ultralytics YOLO detection model, runs it over still images or video, stores every detection as a reviewable region of interest, groups those regions by visual similarity so that a human can accept or reject them in bulk, and exports the result as training data, statistics, image sidecars, and additional metadata. The human review stage is the centre of the design: an annotator can validate, relabel, resize, delete, and draw entirely new localizations, and every one of those edits is written back to the same database the detector wrote to. Video receives particular attention: the software treats each tracker-produced track as a provisional observation and selects one representative frame instead of retaining every detection in the track. We describe the pipeline stage by stage, including the operational middle-third heuristic used for track observation selection.

</details>


### [16] [SBMVTrack: Spike-Budgeted Multi-View Learning for Energy-Efficient UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25503)
*Pengzhi Zhong,Jiwei Mo,Haolun Li,Ge Zheng,Jingqi Wang,Xinyi Bo,Shuiwang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SBMVTrack全脉冲框架，通过能量加权脉冲预算与掩码多视图目标建模，实现更优的精度-能耗权衡的无人机视觉跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN跟踪器仅用脉冲发放率评估能耗，缺乏对实际脉冲活动的显式优化，能耗利用不充分。

Method: 提出Energy-Weighted Spike Budgeting（EWSB），按各脉冲层计算成本加权实际脉冲活动，约束能量加权发放率与饱和活动以减少冗余脉冲计算；并提出Masked Multi-View Target Modeling（MVTM），将初始模板、在线模板与搜索区域视为相关时序视图，通过跨视图特征补全与身份一致性学习增强目标表示鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个基准上，SBMVTrack有效降低平均脉冲发放率与理论能耗，同时保持有竞争力的跟踪性能，取得更优的精度-能耗权衡。

Conclusion: SBMVTrack为节能无人机视觉跟踪提供了一种全脉冲解决方案，验证了显式脉冲活动优化与多视图建模的有效性。源代码将在录用后发布。

Abstract: With sparse and event-driven computation, spiking neural networks show great potential for achieving accurate and energy-efficient UAV visual tracking. However, existing SNN-based trackers typically use spike firing rates only for energy evaluation and lack explicit optimization of actual spike activity. To address this, we propose SBMVTrack, a fully spiking framework for energy-efficient UAV tracking. SBMVTrack introduces Energy-Weighted Spike Budgeting (EWSB). EWSB weights actual spike activity according to the computational cost of each spiking layer. It constrains the energy-weighted firing rate and saturation activity, thereby reducing redundant spike computations. To improve tracking performance under the spike budget constraint, we propose Masked Multi-View Target Modeling (MVTM). This method treats the initial template, online template, and search region from the same sequence as correlated temporal views. It enhances the robustness of target representations through cross-view feature completion and identity-consistency learning. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that SBMVTrack effectively reduces the average spike firing rate and theoretical energy consumption. Meanwhile, it maintains competitive tracking performance, achieving a better accuracy-energy trade-off. The source code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [17] [Real-World Perception for Autonomous Driving in Adverse Weather: Enhancing Standard Detectors via Foundation-Guided Auto-Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25515)
*Sepideh Gohari,Goodarz Mehr,Azim Eskandarian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基础模型引导的自动标注流程，用SAM3离线生成伪标签微调YOLOv8，无需改架构即可提升恶劣天气与光照下的检测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶用标准目标检测器在恶劣天气与光照下性能下降，除非用大量领域数据训练；大规模视觉基础模型虽零样本泛化强，但计算开销大，无法实时部署。

Method: 在自建真实驾驶数据集（覆盖25种场景，含不同路线、天气、光照）上基准测试YOLOv8、Co-DETR、SAM3三种模型；基于结果选择SAM3作为离线自动标注器，为未标注子集生成伪标签，再用其微调基线YOLOv8。

Result: 微调后YOLOv8整体mAP提升16.04%，跨环境稳定性改善；住宅直射阳光场景mAP提升32.73%，高速雾天提升28.65%。

Conclusion: 标准检测器无需大量人工标注或架构修改即可获得环境鲁棒性，基础模型引导的自动标注是实用可行的折中方案。

Abstract: Standard deployment-ready object detectors for autonomous vehicles degrade in adverse weather and lighting conditions without being trained on extensive domain-specific data. While large-scale vision foundation models offer robust zero-shot generalization, their high computational cost makes them impractical for real-time deployment. To bridge this gap, we propose a foundation-guided auto-annotation pipeline that enhances standard detectors without architectural changes. We first benchmark three distinct models, YOLOv8, Co-DETR, and SAM3, on our custom real-world driving dataset spanning 25 unique operational scenarios across various route, weather, and lighting conditions. Based on our analysis, SAM3 demonstrates superior accuracy and resilience across all scenarios. Thus, we deploy it as an offline auto-annotator to generate pseudo-labels on the unannotated subset of our dataset. Fine-tuning the baseline YOLOv8 on these annotations yields a 16.04% higher overall mean Average Precision (mAP) and improves cross-environmental stability compared to the baseline model, highlighted by a 32.73% and 28.65% mAP increase in Residential Direct Sunlight and Highway Fog, respectively. These results demonstrate that standard detectors can achieve environmental resilience without the need for extensive manual annotation or architectural modifications.

</details>


### [18] [Point Diffusion Mamba: Unified Diffusion-State-Space Modeling for Single-View 3D Reconstruction under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25538)
*Wei Zhou,Xinzhe Shi,Xingxing Hao,Xing Hao,Kang Li,Jinye Peng,Ying He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Point Diffusion Mamba（PDM），将扩散模型的生成能力与状态空间模型的效率结合，用于数据稀缺条件下的单视图3D重建，在ShapeNet和Pix3D上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视图3D重建从本身存在歧义的2D观测中推断复杂3D结构本质上是病态问题，尤其在数据稀缺这一研究不足的场景下更具挑战性。

Method: PDM包含面向无序点云输入的轻量重建模块；通过局部几何聚合模块与Mamba块联合建模全局几何结构与局部细节；引入层次化特征整合网络融合高层语义与局部几何特征；并提出动态加权采样策略，利用生成先验自适应统一3D生成与单视图重建。

Result: 在ShapeNet和Pix3D基准上，PDM性能优于当前最先进方法，为数据稀缺场景下的3D重建提供了有效方案。代码已开源。

Conclusion: PDM通过融合扩散生成能力与状态空间模型效率，有效缓解了数据稀缺下单视图3D重建的病态性问题，验证了生成先验辅助重建的有效性。

Abstract: While single-view 3D reconstruction has seen significant progress, extrapolating complex 3D structures from inherently ambiguous 2D observations remains fundamentally ill-posed, particularly in the critically underexplored data-scarce regime. To address this challenge, we propose Point Diffusion Mamba (PDM), a method that integrates the generative power of diffusion models with the efficiency of state-space model for single-view 3D reconstruction under data-scarce conditions. Specifically, PDM employs a lightweight reconstruction module tailored to handle unordered point-cloud inputs effectively. By combining a Local Geometric Aggregation module with Mamba blocks, our approach jointly models global geometric structures and local details. In 3D reconstruction, each point in the initial noisy input requires a precise prediction, yet the high-level features extracted by the Mamba module capture only abstract semantic information from sparse points. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Hierarchical Feature Integration Network, which fuses high-level semantic and local geometric features for each point, overcoming the limitations of token-based point-cloud reconstruction. Furthermore, we propose a Dynamic Weighted Sampling strategy that adaptively unifies 3D generation with single-view reconstruction by leveraging generative priors to enhance reconstruction quality. Experimental results on the ShapeNet and Pix3D benchmarks demonstrate that PDM outperforms state-of-the-art methods, providing an effective solution for 3D reconstruction under data-scarce settings. Code is available at: https://github.com/NWUzhouwei/PDM.

</details>


### [19] [RootQuantV2: Adapting a Vision Foundation Model for Root-Trait Regression from Minirhizotron Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25567)
*Kinjalk Parth,Sebastian Varela,Andrew D. B. Leakey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RootQuantV2 用冻结的 DINOv3 ViT-L/16 加混合参数高效微调，直接从整幅微根管图像回归预测根长与根表面积，仅训练 3.78% 参数即超越 RootQuant。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 田间作物根系高通量表型方案缺失，制约了对地下性状与过程的理解和改良。微根管是田间无损根系表型的标准方法，但规模化自动性状估计缺乏训练数据；人工标注常锁在专有软件中，仅能导出每图根长与表面积的标量总量。然而这类根系性状的大规模数值档案已经存在，需要一种能直接利用这些数值档案的方法。

Method: 延续 RootQuant 用回归从整幅图像直接预测性状、省去人工描摹掩膜的思路，RootQuantV2 将其 CNN 骨干替换为自监督 ViT。具体做法是采用冻结的 DINOv3 ViT-L/16，并配合混合参数高效微调方案，仅训练 11.9M 参数（占模型 3.78%）。

Result: 根长和面积的 R² 分别达到 0.950 和 0.930；相较 RootQuant，根长与面积的 RMSE 分别降低 24.3% 和 20.7%。

Conclusion: RootQuantV2 使遗留的数值档案得以被重新利用，实现高通量、自动化的根系性状估计。

Abstract: A lack of high-throughput phenotyping solutions for root traits in field-grown crops has severely constrained understanding and improvement of below-ground traits and processes. Minirhizotrons are the standard non-destructive root-phenotyping method in field environments. Computer vision solutions are needed to allow automated trait estimation at scale, but training data is scarce and human annotations are often inaccessible because they reside in proprietary software that only exports per-image scalar totals of root length and surface area. Nevertheless, large numeric archives of these root traits already exist. RootQuant showed that the traits can be predicted directly from the whole image by regression, thus removing manually traced masks from the pipeline; RootQuantV2 takes that idea further by replacing RootQuant's CNN backbone with a self-supervised ViT. We adapt a frozen DINOv3 ViT-L/16 with a hybrid parameter-efficient scheme. Training only 11.9M parameters (3.78% of the model), RootQuantV2 achieves length and area $R^2$ of 0.950 and 0.930, respectively, while lowering length/area RMSE by 24.3%/20.7% over RootQuant. RootQuantV2 thus repurposes legacy numeric archives for high-throughput, automated root trait estimation.

</details>


### [20] [Agentic Building-Aware Satellite Gaussian Splatting for Auditable Urban DSM Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25578)
*Wentao Sun,Zhengsen Xu,Yiping Chen,John S. Zelek,Jonathan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于智能体的建筑感知卫星高斯泼溅工作流，用SAM建筑掩膜作为语义先验，并引入智能体重建控制器来选取、验证和记录DSM重建策略，提升建筑区域与全场景的DSM精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级卫星三维重建服务于灾害响应、城市监测和地理空间数字孪生，但现有神经渲染方法通常只优化平均视觉保真度，而忽略了分析人员最关注的建筑结构。因此需要将重建能力向分析关键区域倾斜。

Method: 以Segment Anything生成的建筑掩膜作为语义先验，构建智能体重建控制器对DSM重建策略进行选择、验证与记录；在建筑区域施加建筑感知加权，并设计分阶段调度方案；跨多个场景输出建筑清册元数据与逐场景决策记录。

Result: 在DFC2019 JAX_004场景上，建筑感知加权将建筑区域DSM MAE从0.844 m降至0.806 m；分阶段调度取得平衡工作点，全场景MAE从1.362 m改善到1.349 m，同时保留建筑增益。在四个JAX场景中，智能体为通用DSM和建筑聚焦DSM目标均选出经过验证的策略。

Conclusion: 该系统整合语义先验、策略选择、区域特定DSM指标与DSM衍生的GIS地表产品，实现了可审计的城市三维分析，表明语义先验能够有效将重建能力导向分析关键区域。

Abstract: Urban-scale 3D reconstruction from satellite imagery supports disaster response, city monitoring, and geospatial digital twins, yet neural rendering methods typically optimize average visual fidelity rather than the structures that analysts inspect first: buildings. We present an agentic building-aware satellite Gaussian Splatting workflow that uses Segment Anything-derived building masks as semantic priors and an Agentic Reconstruction Controller to select, verify, and record DSM reconstruction policies. On the DFC2019 JAX\_004 scene, building-aware weighting reduces building-region DSM MAE from 0.844 m to 0.806 m, showing that semantic priors can shift reconstruction capacity toward analyst-critical regions. A staged schedule provides a balanced operating point, improving full-scene MAE from 1.362 m to 1.349 m while retaining a building gain. Across four JAX scenes, the Agent selects validated policies for both general DSM and building-focused DSM objectives, and produces building-inventory metadata and per-scene decision records. The system combines semantic priors, policy selection, region-specific DSM metrics, and DSM-derived GIS surface products for auditable urban 3D analysis.

</details>


### [21] [Hi-OPD: Hierarchy-Aware Open-Prompt Detection for Remote Sensing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25584)
*Jinlong Hu,Yi Zhang,Zhiqi Xia,Yikang Zhou,Shunping Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出层次感知开放提示检测器Hi-OPD，并构建RS153-HierOPD数据集，通过层次安全的负采样、路径多正样本监督和单向向上一致性训练，解决了扁平开放提示训练下祖先查询无法检索后代实例的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扁平开放提示训练存在一个未被控制的失效模式：当多源遥感标注的粒度不一致且标签缺失时，检测器在原子提示（如car、van）下能定位目标，但在祖先提示（如vehicle）下会漏检同一实例，而扁平AP指标无法暴露这种跨层次不一致性。

Method: 构建RS153-HierOPD数据集（175,644条训练图像/瓦片记录，3.48M框，153个原子类别，含稀疏层次与别名关系）。Hi-OPD采用三项设计：层次安全负采样、路径多正样本监督、单向向上一致性，并以逐源风险排除处理可能缺失的标签。此外，ConvVPE利用检测器原生特征与共享对比头，将K-shot支持框转换为文本兼容嵌入。

Result: Track A上DIOR/DOTA-v2.0达79.7/72.3 AP50，超过OpenRSD的76.7/71.8。受控训练下，完整层次方案将DOTA-v2.0父类AP50从7.2提升至71.5，FAIR1M祖父类AP50从31.6提升至71.4，同时原子AP50从71.4变为72.3。文本路径在三类常见源上达99.7% CAR50（0.3%违规），FAIR1M祖父关系达99.9%/0.1%。留出集VEDAI上文本AP50为75.9，高出OpenRSD 6.2个点。

Conclusion: 联合AP与CAR结果表明，显式层次训练修复了该失效模式，同时保留了原子检测能力与提示迁移能力。

Abstract: Hi-OPD addresses a failure mode left uncontrolled by flat open-prompt training: descendant retrieval need not persist under ancestor queries when multi-source remote sensing annotations exhibit inconsistent granularity and missing labels. A detector may localize \textit{car} and \textit{van} under atomic prompts yet miss the same instances under \textit{vehicle}; flat AP does not expose this cross-level inconsistency.
  We propose Hi-OPD, a hierarchy-aware open-prompt detector, and construct RS153-HierOPD from 175,644 retained training image/tile records and 3.48M boxes mapped to 153 atomic categories with sparse hierarchy and alias relations. Hi-OPD learns ancestor retrieval through hierarchy-safe negative sampling, path multi-positive supervision, and one-way upward consistency, while per-source risk exclusion handles potentially missing labels. ConvVPE converts K-shot support boxes into text-compatible embeddings using detector-native features and the shared contrastive head.
  On Track A, Hi-OPD obtains 79.7/72.3 AP50 on DIOR/DOTA-v2.0, above the literature-reported OpenRSD results of 76.7/71.8. Under controlled training on the original converted annotations, the full hierarchy recipe raises DOTA-v2.0 parent AP50 from 7.2 to 71.5 and FAIR1M grandparent AP50 from 31.6 to 71.4, while DOTA-v2.0 atomic AP50 changes from 71.4 to 72.3. The text path reaches 99.7% CAR50 (0.3% violation) across the three common sources and 99.9%/0.1% on FAIR1M grandparent relations. On held-out VEDAI, text AP50 is 75.9, 6.2 points above OpenRSD. Joint AP and CAR show that explicit hierarchy training repairs this failure mode while retaining atomic detection and prompt transfer.

</details>


### [22] [Observer Choice and Threshold Selection in Retinal Vessel Segmentation: A Subject-Separated Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25597)
*Wenhao Xu,Yixian Kong,Ting Pan,Changwei Wang,Feilong Wang,Rongtao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究考察视网膜血管分割中阈值选择策略对评价结果的影响，发现maximin调优并未带来准确率收益，主张明确报告阈值选择参考与评价参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割阈值的标注来源属于评价协议的一部分，但其影响容易被误认为模型质量差异。作者希望厘清阈值选择这一环节在视网膜血管分割评价中的实际作用。

Method: 使用CHASE DB1全部28张图像及两份人工标注，采用固定七折协议（同一受试者双眼保持同折）。以观察者1为目标训练随机森林与Extra Trees，三种随机种子共42次拟合。五种阈值策略共享相同得分图：固定0.50、观察者1调优、观察者2调优、平均观察者调优、以及对逐图较低观察者Dice的maximin调优。

Result: 随机森林中maximin在21次拟合里有19次改变阈值，但最差观察者Dice从70.53%降至70.45%，配对差为-0.073个百分点，条件受试者自助法95%区间为[-0.384, 0.238]。Extra Trees方向一致。同一组观察者1调优的随机森林掩膜对观察者1得73.66%，对观察者2仅71.06%。

Conclusion: 结果支持明确报告阈值选择参考与评价参考，但不支持在本队列中从maximin调优获得准确率收益。全部划分、原始预测、指标与代码均已公开，并披露了AI辅助。

Abstract: The annotation used to select a segmentation threshold is part of the evaluation protocol, yet its effect is easily conflated with model quality. We examine this choice for retinal vessel segmentation using all 28 CHASE DB1 images and both human annotations. A fixed seven-fold protocol keeps both eyes of each of the 14 subjects together. Random forests and Extra Trees are fitted against observer 1 with three random seeds, yielding 42 fits. Five threshold policies share identical score maps: fixed 0.50, observer-1 tuning, observer-2 tuning, mean-observer tuning, and maximin tuning of the per-image lower observer Dice. For random forests, maximin changes the threshold in 19 of 21 fits, but worst-observer Dice decreases from 70.53 percent to 70.45 percent. The paired difference is -0.073 percentage points, with a conditional subject-bootstrap 95 percent interval of [-0.384, 0.238]. Extra Trees shows the same direction. Identical observer-1-tuned random-forest masks score 73.66 percent against observer 1 and 71.06 percent against observer 2. The results support explicit reporting of both the threshold-selection reference and evaluation reference; they do not support an accuracy benefit from maximin tuning in this cohort. All splits, raw predictions, metrics and code are supplied. AI assistance is disclosed.

</details>


### [23] [Ultra-fast Neural Inference for Stochastic Gaussian Splatting Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25604)
*Chenxiao Hu,Hao Zhang,Yanchen Zhang,Meng Gai,Guoping Wang,Sheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时序神经去噪器，用于消除随机高斯泼溅渲染中的空间噪声，实现时间稳定的自由视角导航，同时保留免排序、免混合的光栅化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机渲染消除了高斯泼溅中的排序和alpha混合过程，但代价是引入了空间噪声。作者希望在保持免排序、免混合光栅化性能优势的同时，去除这种噪声。

Method: 在视图一致的随机泼溅渲染器共享的像素流上构建时序去噪问题，提出时序神经去噪器，并在随机2D高斯泼溅渲染上验证。方法结合了双路径指数移动平均累积、逐像素学习信任预测用于历史验证、固定各向异性空间滤波器，以及带稳定化的方差门控合成。

Result: 去噪器抑制了噪声，在自由相机导航中实现了时间稳定、视觉上令人信服的输出，同时保留了免排序、免混合的光栅化性能。整个管线与排序alpha混合渲染器相比仍存在PSNR差距，但去噪器的开销低于去除排序和混合所节省的时间。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过时序神经去噪可以在保留免排序、免混合光栅化性能优势的前提下，有效抑制随机高斯泼溅渲染的空间噪声，实现时间稳定的高质量渲染输出。

Abstract: Stochastic rendering eliminates the sorting and alpha blending process in Gaussian splatting, at the cost of introducing spatial noise. Formulating temporal denoising over the pixel stream shared by view-consistent stochastic splatting renderers, we propose a temporal neural denoiser validated on stochastic 2D Gaussian Splatting rendering, combining dual-path exponential moving average accumulation, per-pixel learned trust prediction for history validation, a fixed anisotropic spatial filter and a variance-gated composition with stabilization. The denoiser suppresses the noise, achieving temporally stable, visually compelling outputs during free camera navigation, all while retaining the sort-free, blend-free rasterization performance. The combined pipeline retains a PSNR gap to sorted alpha-blending renderers, but the denoiser's overhead stays below the time saved by removing sorting and blending.

</details>


### [24] [Evidence-gated multimodal parsing and vectorization of architectural floor plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25615)
*Hongxuan Chen,Wenda Wang,Jiachen Lu,Qirui Shen,Zilong Huang,Lei He,Xinyue Dong,Weixin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SALI-FP是一种证据门控的多模态流水线，可将建筑平面图转换为可复核的语义图、对象、矢量与关系记录，并在大规模生产审计中实现了全量结构化输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑平面图因异构图元同时编码空间语义与可编辑几何，成为档案数字化与早期设计建模准备中的高摩擦障碍。现有方式缺乏对本地修改的图像证据约束，难以直接用于CAD/BIM准备。

Method: 提出SALI-FP，采用证据门控的多模态流水线，将平面图解析为语义图、对象、矢量及关系记录，并利用图像证据约束局部修订。公开基准校准与附录F中30例匹配视觉证据集用于直接检查房间尺度覆盖、开口、斜向边界与流线连续性。

Result: 在11,534张异构平面图的全生产审计中，SALI-FP为每张平面图都生成了结构化输出，包含752,510个有效带多边形对象。同一输出形式已支持实际设计工作中的图纸数字化与设计建模准备。

Conclusion: SALI-FP为可复核的CAD/BIM准备与既有建筑信息恢复提供了一种工程导向的“解释到几何”工作流。

Abstract: Architectural floor plans remain a high-friction barrier to archive digitization and early design-model preparation because heterogeneous graphics encode spatial semantics and editable geometry together. We introduce SALI-FP, an evidence-gated multimodal pipeline that converts a plan into reviewable semantic maps, objects, vectors, and relation records while constraining local revisions by image evidence. In a full production audit of 11,534 heterogeneous plans, SALI-FP produced structured outputs for every plan, including 752,510 valid polygon-bearing objects. The same output form has supported initial drawing digitization and design-model preparation in practical design work. Public-benchmark calibration is paired with a 30-case matched visual evidence set in Appendix F, where room-scale coverage, openings, oblique boundaries, and circulation continuity can be inspected directly. SALI-FP offers an engineering-oriented interpretation-to-geometry workflow for reviewed CAD/BIM preparation and existing-building information recovery.

</details>


### [25] [Shallow to Deep: Aligning Token Pruning with Stage-wise Roles in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25635)
*Shuo Zhang,Jintao Tong,Yixiong Zou,Yuhua Li,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STD分层token剪枝框架，按网络阶段功能自适应选择token，在保持甚至提升性能的同时大幅减少视觉token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM因冗余视觉token导致高计算成本；现有免训练基于注意力的多层剪枝在浅层剪枝会持续损害性能，需理解该问题并寻求解决方案。

Method: 分析各深度注意力模式：浅层为边缘检测、注意力图混乱；深层经历局部主体识别与不稳定语义聚合。据此提出STD分层剪枝框架：浅层用高频谱分析确定性保留结构边缘；中间层用高斯平滑注意力保持空间连贯；深层引入稳定性自适应触发器，仅在语义稳定阶段剪枝。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5-7B上以88.9% token减少率超越SOTA剪枝方法1.1%；在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上以94.4%减少率提升2.1%，预填充阶段加速3.9倍，且可插拔、可与其他方法结合。

Conclusion: 剪枝策略应与网络各阶段功能对齐，STD通过分层自适应机制有效降低计算成本并保持性能。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) incur high computational costs from redundant visual tokens. Although training-free attention-based multi-layer pruning in the vision encoder stage has been explored as an effective strategy, we find that pruning in shallow layers consistently degrades performance. In this paper, we aim to understand this problem and seek a solution. By analyzing attention patterns across network depth, we find that shallow layers primarily function as edge detectors with chaotic attention maps, while deeper layers transition through local subject recognition and unstable semantic aggregation. To address the misalignment between pruning strategies and network stages, we propose STD, a hierarchical token pruning framework that adapts token selection mechanisms to the functional role of each network stage. STD employs High-Frequency Spectral Analysis in shallow layers to deterministically preserve structural edges, uses Gaussian-Smoothed Attention in intermediate layers to maintain spatial coherence, and introduces a Stability-Adaptive Trigger in deep layers to execute pruning only during semantically stable phases. Extensive experiments show that STD outperforms state-of-the-art pruning methods by 1.1% on LLaVA-1.5-7B with 88.9% token reduction, while also being plug-and-play and highly effective when combined with other methods, and by 2.1% on LLaVA-NeXT-7B with 94.4% reduction, delivering a 3.9x speed-up in the prefilling stage. Our code will be released at https://github.com/Twilight03/STD.

</details>


### [26] [What Drives Hierarchy-Aware Image Retrieval? Taxonomy Alignment, Objective Choice, and Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25638)
*Ling Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在冻结DINOv2特征上，严格层次检索的提升主要来自层级感知监督目标（Regression→Taxonomy-SupCon），而非欧氏/双曲几何选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高层类别检索准确率高，并不代表嵌入尊重目标语义分类体系；需要区分层次检索提升中"监督组织"与"几何选择"各自的贡献。

Method: 采用严格的跨类准则（排除更细粒度匹配）评估高层级；对比欧氏与双曲投影，配合分类距离回归或层级感知监督对比（SupCon）目标；使用计算匹配的2×2 Geometry×Loss因子设计（相同768-256-32投影容量、优化日程、批序、100轮预算），并在NABirds上做曲率/半径控制及语义对齐控制（真实分类体系 vs 结构保持的打乱层级）。

Result: CUB上，目标族对比在平均层次mAP上为欧氏+0.0487、双曲+0.0414，而几何对比仅+0.0102、+0.0030；NABirds Parent-disjoint检索中目标族对比为+0.0467、+0.0440，几何对比为+0.0017、-0.0009。真实分类体系显著优于打乱层级；曲率/半径控制不支持更强负曲率作为解释。

Conclusion: 总体而言，Regression→Taxonomy-SupCon的目标族对比大于几何对比；语义对齐本身也有独立作用，而几何效应依赖具体层次结构。

Abstract: Foundation vision models provide strong generic representations, yet high class-level retrieval accuracy does not necessarily imply that an embedding respects a target semantic taxonomy. We study strict explicit-taxonomy image retrieval on frozen DINOv2 features and ask: when hierarchical retrieval improves, how much of the change is associated with the organization of taxonomy-aware supervision, and how much with the Euclidean-hyperbolic geometry choice?
  We evaluate higher levels with strict cross-class criteria that exclude finer-grained matches, and compare Euclidean and hyperbolic projections trained with taxonomy-distance regression or a taxonomy-aware supervised contrastive objective. A compute-matched 2 x 2 Geometry x Loss factorial uses the same 768-256-32 projector capacity, optimization schedule, batch order, and fixed 100-epoch budget; the Loss axis denotes the Regression-to-Taxonomy-SupCon objective-family contrast. On CUB, the objective-family contrasts in mean hierarchy mAP (strict middle/high average, excluding Class/Leaf) are +0.0487 in Euclidean space and +0.0414 in hyperbolic space, compared with geometry contrasts of +0.0102 and +0.0030. On NABirds Parent-disjoint retrieval, the corresponding objective-family contrasts are +0.0467 and +0.0440, whereas geometry contrasts are +0.0017 and -0.0009.
  A semantic-alignment control shows that the true taxonomy substantially outperforms a structure-preserving shuffled hierarchy, while a NABirds curvature/radius control does not support stronger negative curvature as the explanation for the observed hierarchy gains. Across the two taxonomies, the Regression-to-Taxonomy-SupCon contrasts are larger in aggregate than the evaluated geometry contrasts; semantic alignment also matters separately, while geometry remains hierarchy-dependent.

</details>


### [27] [Decoupling Disease, Covariates, and Individual Variability: A Unified Disentanglement Framework for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25650)
*Shengjie Zhang,Jinglin Zhang,Zhuangzhuang Jiang,Ziqi Yu,Yipin Zhang,Qi Zhang,Xiang Chen,Haibo Yang,Fei Gao,Longbiao Cui,Yuan Zhou,Xiao-Yong Zhang,Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MedIDL框架，通过三个相互正交的潜空间解耦疾病特征、协变量和个体差异，在7个医学影像数据集上取得最优分类精度，并验证了表征隔离与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类中，准确分离疾病相关特征与混杂协变量（如年龄、性别、站点）及个体变异是根本挑战。传统基于回归的方法可能忽略图像特征与真实协变量之间的非线性关系。

Method: 提出广义医学影像解耦学习（MedIDL）框架，将图像特征映射到三个相互正交的潜空间，对应三个专门的解耦头：由监督损失引导的疾病分类头、受跨被试相似度匹配约束的协变量对齐头，以及吸收个体变异的Gaussian头。

Result: 在涵盖多种成像模态的7个数据集上评估，MedIDL在所有数据集上的准确率均优于最先进的监督与自监督分类方法。关联分析表明其成功隔离了目标特异的潜表征，基于梯度的可解释性映射定位出与既有临床文献一致的优势病变模式。

Conclusion: MedIDL通过正交潜空间解耦有效解决了医学图像分类中疾病特征与混杂因素的分离问题，兼具性能优势与可解释性。

Abstract: Accurately isolating disease-related features from confounding covariates (e.g., age, gender, site) and individual variations remains a fundamental challenge in medical image classification. Traditional regression-based approaches may ignore non-linear relations between image features and true covariates. To overcome this issue, we present a generalized Medical Imaging Disentanglement Learning (MedIDL) framework. MedIDL maps image features into three mutually orthogonal latent spaces through specialized disentanglement heads: a disease classification head guided by a supervised loss, a covariate-alignment head constrained by cross-subject similarity matching, and a Gaussian head absorbing individual variations. We evaluated our framework across 7 datasets encompassing diverse imaging modalities. MedIDL outperforms state-of-the-art supervised and self-supervised classification methods in accuracy across all datasets. Association analyses demonstrate that MedIDL successfully isolates target-specific latent representations. Gradient-based interpretability mappings localize pathognomonic patterns aligning with established clinical literature.

</details>


### [28] [GameDirector: Decoupling Gameplay Logic from Rendering for Player-Configurable Game World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25652)
*Zijun Lin,Zhiyang Deng,Yuzhe Wu,Bihan Wen,Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GameDirector 首次提出一种智能体框架，将基于规则的游戏玩法逻辑与视觉渲染解耦，在保留连贯游戏机制的同时支持玩家自定义配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏世界模型依赖像素级监督、端到端联合建模感知、记忆、状态转移与渲染，能实现开放式可操控生成，但难以可靠执行健康扣减、技能激活、战斗规则、终止条件等显式游戏机制。游戏引擎虽能通过硬编码规则保证这些机制，却牺牲了玩家驱动的创作灵活性。因此需要在这两种范式之间搭建桥梁。

Method: 框架将规则化游戏逻辑与视觉渲染分离，充当"智能导演"：解析视觉观察、更新游戏状态、战术性地控制 NPC 并强制执行玩法规则，再把决策转化为文本提示，引导视频世界模型渲染相应玩法。玩家可像游戏开发者一样配置角色、状态与规则。

Result: 在三个游戏上、使用自动化玩法智能体采集的数据进行实验，GameDirector 实现了准确的状态跟踪和可靠的规则遵循，并使 Boss 动作质量相比多种端到端游戏世界模型设置提升超过 39.9%。

Conclusion: 通过外化玩家可控的游戏逻辑，GameDirector 在硬编码仿真与生成式建模之间建立了中间地带，实现了更灵活、闭环的玩法体验。

Abstract: Recent game world models support realistic visual simulation and interactive gameplay based on player inputs. However, they typically learn environment dynamics from pixel-level supervision, jointly modeling perception, memory, state transitions, and rendering within a single end-to-end framework. While this design enables open-ended, action-controllable generation, it still falls short of delivering a complete gameplay experience. Games are governed by explicit mechanics, such as health deduction, skill activation, combat rules, and termination conditions. These mechanics depend on precise and consistent state transitions that generative models alone cannot reliably enforce. In contrast, game engines can guarantee such mechanics through hard-coded rules, but provide limited flexibility for player-driven creation. To bridge these paradigms, we introduce GameDirector, the first agentic framework that decouples rule-based gameplay logic from visual rendering. Given player-defined configurations, the framework acts as an intelligent director that interprets visual observations, updates game states, tactically controls NPCs, and enforces gameplay rules. It then translates these decisions into text prompts that guide the video world model to render the resulting gameplay. This separation allows players to configure characters, states, and rules much like a game developer while preserving coherent game mechanics. Experiments on three games, using data collected by our automated gameplay agent, show that GameDirector achieves accurate state tracking, reliable rule following, and improves boss action quality by more than 39.9% over various end-to-end game world model settings. Overall, by externalizing player-controllable game logic, GameDirector establishes a middle ground between hard-coded simulation and generative modeling, enabling more flexible and closed-loop gameplay experiences.

</details>


### [29] [Real-Time Atomic-Resolution Electron Phase Imaging without Probe Calibration via Ptychography-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25684)
*H. Yue,C. -C. Chen,C. -N. Hsiao,J. Cheng,Y. Liu,X. Z. Liao,Steve F. Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种叠层衍射成像监督的局部推理框架，将4D-STEM转为可边采边算的原子级相位成像流程，单探针位置延迟约0.27 ms，吞吐约2万点/秒，比GPU加速ePIE快约1000倍，并具跨材料、跨离焦、跨仪器泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子级相位成像对解析纳米材料中的缺陷、界面和弱散射原子至关重要。电子叠层衍射成像虽能提供亚埃级相位灵敏度，但其迭代重建计算开销大且对实验标定敏感，只能离线进行，无法在数据采集过程中实时使用。

Method: 以单个实验AuPd数据集重建的物理约束参考相位图作为教师标签，训练一个紧凑模型，直接从衍射测量预测局部相位块，无需显式探针输入或在线迭代优化；再通过确定性重叠拼接组装全视场图像。

Result: 在线延迟约0.27 ms/探针位置，吞吐约20,000位置/秒，较GPU加速ePIE约提速1000倍，同时保持原子级晶格衬度和倒易空间保真度；无需微调即可迁移至WS2材料、高熵合金纳米颗粒离焦条件以及在300 kV下的hBN仪器。

Conclusion: 该方法将叠层衍射的冗余性摊销为一个快速、可泛化的工作流，实现材料显微学的实时原子级相位成像。

Abstract: Atomic-scale phase imaging is central to resolving defects, interfaces, and weakly scattering atoms that govern the behavior of nanoscale materials. Electron ptychography delivers sub-ångström phase sensitivity but remains an offline technique, because its iterative reconstruction is computationally expensive and sensitive to experimental calibration, preventing live use during data acquisition. Here, a ptychography-supervised local inference framework is presented that converts four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D-STEM) into an acquisition-compatible phase-imaging workflow. Physics-constrained reference phase maps reconstructed from a single experimental AuPd dataset serve as teacher labels for a compact model that predicts local phase patches directly from diffraction measurements, without explicit probe input or online iterative optimization. Full-field images are assembled by deterministic overlap stitching. The workflow reaches an online latency of about 0.27 ms per probe position and a throughput of about 20,000 positions per second, an approximately 1,000-fold speed-up over GPU-accelerated ePIE, while preserving atomic-scale lattice contrast and reciprocal-space fidelity. Without fine-tuning, the same model transfers across materials (WS2), defocus conditions (high-entropy alloy nanoparticles), and instruments (hBN at 300 kV). The approach amortizes ptychographic redundancy into a fast, generalizable workflow that enables real-time atomic-scale phase imaging for materials microscopy.

</details>


### [30] [Initialization and Stopping Tolerance in CPU Dermoscopic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25685)
*Wenhao Xu,Yixian Kong,Ting Pan,Changwei Wang,Feilong Wang,Rongtao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 轮廓初始化与停止容差共同影响活动轮廓分割评估：Otsu 初始化相对棋盘初始化提升 Dice，但仍劣于 Otsu 阈值基线，且该优势随容差收紧和病灶大小变化而反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活动轮廓分割的性能评估常忽视初始化方式与数值停止条件之间的交互作用，可能导致对方法改进的错误解读，因此需要系统考察二者对评估结果的影响。

Method: 在调整尺寸的 ISIC 2017 镜像数据上，使用 scikit-image 的 Chan-Vese 实现。用 100 张固定开发集图像选定统一输入通道，再评估全部 600 张留出图像。对比 Otsu 阈值法与棋盘、圆盘、Otsu 初始化轮廓，在默认与更严格水平集容差下运行。

Result: 默认容差下，Otsu 初始化将平均 Dice 从棋盘的 0.6011 提升至 0.6660（配对差 0.0649，95% 自助区间 [0.0452, 0.0860]），但 Otsu 阈值单独可达 0.6897。默认圆盘初始化在 471 张图像上仅迭代一次即停止。收紧容差后 Otsu 相对棋盘的优势降至 0.0197，多数运行触及 500 次迭代上限；默认容差优势在小/大病灶分层间也发生反转。

Conclusion: 相对通用初始化器的改进可能与相对阈值基线的退化同时存在。评估应保留未精化掩膜作为对照，并同时报告初始场定义、停止容差与观测迭代次数。

Abstract: Contour initialization and numerical stopping can jointly affect the evaluation of active-contour segmentation. We examine their interaction using the open-source scikit-image Chan-Vese implementation on a resized ISIC 2017 mirror. A fixed development set of 100 images selects a common input channel; all 600 images in the repository's held-out partition are then evaluated. Otsu thresholding is compared with checkerboard-, disk-, and Otsu-initialized contours under default and tighter level-set tolerances. At the default tolerance, Otsu initialization increases mean image Dice from 0.6011 to 0.6660 relative to checkerboard initialization, a paired difference of 0.0649 (95% image-bootstrap interval [0.0452, 0.0860]). Otsu thresholding alone achieves 0.6897. The default disk initializer stops after one iteration on 471 images. Tightening the tolerance reduces the Otsu-seed advantage over checkerboard initialization to 0.0197, with most runs reaching the 500-iteration limit. The default-tolerance advantage also reverses between small- and large-lesion strata. These findings show that an improvement over a generic initializer can coexist with deterioration relative to the threshold baseline. Evaluations should retain the unrefined mask as a comparator and report the initial-field definition, stopping tolerance, and observed iteration counts together.

</details>


### [31] [C2FXNet: Coarse-to-Fine Scene Expert for Unified Object Detection across Adverse Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25693)
*Tianle Fang,Zhenbing Liu,Chong Yin,Bolun Li,Haoxiang Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出C2FXNet，通过粗到细的分层场景引导实现统一恶劣天气目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣天气下的目标检测因严重退化削弱视觉质量并破坏跨场景语义特征而具挑战性，现有方法多依赖特定条件设计，难以在统一检测器中泛化。

Method: C2FXNet采用双层引导机制：多步推理路由器（MRR）基于GRU对压缩的多尺度视觉线索与冻结的粗场景原型进行循环场景推理；细场景精炼（FSR）模块利用图像特定语义线索调制高层特征以应对局部变化；场景感知混合专家（SMoE）在MRR与FSR联合引导下动态组合场景专家。

Result: 在RTTS、ExDark及新构建的恶劣天气数据集（AWD）上，C2FXNet在雾、暗、清晰条件下均持续优于现有方法，分别达到63.70%、71.14%和54.19% mAP。

Conclusion: 通过耦合粗场景推理与细粒度语义精炼，C2FXNet在无需场景特定训练的情况下实现了鲁棒的多场景检测。

Abstract: Object detection in adverse weather remains challenging because severe degradations weaken visual quality and disrupt semantic feature representations across diverse scenes. Existing methods usually rely on condition-specific designs, which limits their ability to generalize within a unified detector. In this paper, we propose a Coarse-to-Fine Scene Expert Network (C2FXNet) that achieves unified detection through hierarchical scene guidance. Specifically, C2FXNet introduces a dual-level guidance mechanism consisting of a Multi-step Reasoning Router (MRR), which performs GRU-based recurrent scene reasoning over compressed multi-scale visual cues and frozen coarse scene prototypes, and a Fine Scene Refinement (FSR) module, which uses image-specific semantic cues to modulate high-level features for local variation handling. Furthermore, a Scene-aware Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) dynamically combines scene-specific experts under the joint guidance of MRR and FSR. By coupling coarse scene reasoning with fine-grained semantic refinement, C2FXNet enables robust multi-scene detection without scene-specific training. Extensive experiments on RTTS, ExDark, and our newly constructed Adverse Weather Dataset (AWD) demonstrate that C2FXNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across foggy, dark, and clear conditions, reaching 63.70%, 71.14%, and 54.19% mAP on RTTS, ExDark, and AWD, respectively. The source code will be released at https://github.com/PolarisFTL/C2FXNet.

</details>


### [32] [Interpretable AI plus Handheld, Portable Retinal Photographs: A Low-Cost Glaucoma Screening Solution for West Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25697)
*Charis Y. N. Chiang,Tarela Sarimiye,Adeyinka Ashaye,Martin Buist,Michael A. Hauser,Olusola Olawoye,Michaël J. A. Girard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究开发并评估了一套可解释的AI框架，用于在尼日利亚社区人群中使用低成本手持视网膜相机（Volk Viva）进行青光眼筛查，并与台式相机（Canon CR-2-AF）进行性能比较。结果显示两类设备上的组件模型表现均良好，分类AUC分别为0.85±0.01和0.93±0.01，表明可解释AI结合低成本便携成像有望提升社区级青光眼筛查能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青光眼的早期筛查在资源有限、专科医生匮乏的地区难以普及。低成本便携式视网膜成像设备有望弥补这一缺口，但其图像质量与临床台式设备的可比性，以及AI模型在该类图像上的可解释性和可靠性尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 研究纳入尼日利亚社区681名参与者（1,362只眼）：414只青光眼、478只疑似青光眼、470只非青光眼。分别使用Volk Viva手持相机和Canon CR-2-AF台式相机采集眼底照片。针对每种设备分别微调组件模型，完成血管分割、视杯视盘边界分割和视神经乳头特征提取；再通过最终分类模型将扫描结果分为青光眼、疑似青光眼或非青光眼。采用特征权重分析和Grad-CAM进行模型可解释性分析。

Result: 模型在两种设备图像上均表现良好：血管分割Dice系数为0.98（Volk）和0.94（Canon）；视杯视盘分割为0.95（Volk）和0.96（Canon）；视神经乳头特征检测AUC为0.83±0.03（Volk）和0.87±0.04（Canon）；分类模型AUC为0.85±0.01（Volk）和0.93±0.01（Canon）。每张图像的报告中提供模型决策置信度评分和决策依据可视化，以支持临床解读。

Conclusion: Volk Viva在组件模型上与Canon CR-2-AF具有合理可比性，在分类任务上差距不大。这表明可解释AI与低成本便携成像相结合，可能有助于提升社区层面的青光眼筛查，尤其在专科医疗资源可及性有限的地区具有应用价值。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate an interpretable artificial intelligence (AI) framework for glaucoma screening from low-cost portable, handheld retinal fundus photographs in a West African population and to compare its performance with clinical tabletop fundus imaging. Methods: We used data from a community-based study of 681 participants (1,362 eyes) in Nigeria, comprising 414 glaucoma, 478 glaucoma suspect, and 470 non-glaucoma eyes. Fundus photographs were acquired using the low-cost handheld, portable Volk Viva retinal camera and the Canon CR-2-AF tabletop camera. We fine-tuned component models separately to each device to perform vessel segmentation, cup and disc boundary segmentation, and feature extraction to detect optic nerve head features. A final classification model combined these components to classify scans as glaucoma, glaucoma suspect or non-glaucoma. Feature-weight analysis and Gradient-weighted Class Activation Mapping were used for interpretation. Results: The models performed well on both Volk Viva and Canon CR-2-AF images: Vessel segmentation: 0.98 Dice Coefficient (DC) (Volk) and 0.94 DC (Canon); Cup and disc segmentation: 0.95 DC (Volk) and 0.96 DC (Canon); Optic nerve head feature detection: area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUCs) of 0.83$\pm$0.03 (Volk) and 0.87$\pm$0.04 (Canon); Classification model: AUCs of 0.85$\pm$0.01 (Volk) and 0.93$\pm$0.01 (Canon). Reports for each image, present model decision confidence scores and decision-rationale visualizations to support clinical interpretation. Conclusions: Volk Viva results were reasonably comparable to Canon CR-2-AF in the component models and not far behind in classification. This shows that interpretable AI combined with low-cost, portable imaging may enhance community-level glaucoma screening, especially in settings with limited specialist access and resources.

</details>


### [33] [FoMo: Forking Moment in Generative Trajectory as a Perceptual Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25716)
*Jaihyun Lew,Mingi Jung,Minjun Park,Wooseok Song,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全自动数据生成流程 FoMo，利用扩散模型生成轨迹中的“分叉时刻”作为感知距离标签，无需人工标注即可训练参考型图像质量评估（IQA）指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考型IQA指标的训练高度依赖人工标注数据。基于MOS的逐点打分标注成本高、噪声大；2AFC成对标签虽可靠高效，却只能提供相对比较信息，缺乏逐点感知距离标签。因此需要一种无需人工标注、能生成逐点感知距离标签的自动化方案。

Method: 作者提出FoMo流程：利用扩散模型生成过程的动态特性作为感知距离代理。由于图像粗结构在早期时间步生成、细细节在后期生成，在生成较早阶段分叉的两张图像只共享粗结构，感知距离大；在较晚阶段分叉则仅细节不同，感知距离小。以分叉时刻作为参考锚定的距离标签，监督训练参考型IQA指标，从而获得支持任意图像对通用比较的逐点标签。

Result: 在多种主干网络架构上的广泛实验验证了该生成流程的有效性，并在多个基准测试上超越使用人工标注数据集训练的结果。

Conclusion: 扩散轨迹与人类视觉系统的感知方式高度对齐，分叉时刻可作为有效的感知距离标签。该全自动流程为参考型IQA提供了一种信息丰富、无需人工标注的训练范式，并优于人工标注数据集。

Abstract: Reference-based image quality assessment (IQA) metrics aim to reflect how humans perceive the perceptual distance between a pair of images. To learn how the human visual system (HVS) operates, recent reference-based IQA metrics heavily rely on human-annotated data. Mean opinion score (MOS)-based pointwise scoring, which assigns a scalar quality value per image, is preferable for annotation but is prohibitively expensive to collect at scale and is known to be noisy due to inconsistent human judgments. As an alternative, two-alternative forced choice (2AFC) pairwise labels have gained popularity due to their reliability and efficiency, but they capture only relative comparisons between pairs. In this paper, we propose a fully automated data generation pipeline that generates pointwise perceptual distance labels between image pairs without any human annotation. Our approach exploits the generative dynamics of diffusion models as a perceptual distance proxy, where the coarse structure of an image is generated in the early timesteps and the fine details are generated in the later timesteps. Images that fork early in the generation process share only coarse structure and are perceptually far apart; images that fork late differ only in fine detail. We demonstrate that the diffusion trajectory aligns well with the human visual system, and use this forking moment, FoMo, as a reference-grounded distance label to supervise the training of a reference-based IQA metric. The pointwise labels, which support universal comparison between arbitrary image pairs, enable an information-rich training objective. Extensive experiments across diverse backbone architectures confirm the effectiveness of our generation pipeline, outperforming human-annotated datasets in multiple benchmarks.

</details>
